## Corpus Check
- 1887 files · ~2,299,481 words

## Summary
- 36650 nodes · 64888 edges · 1441 communities (1203 shown, 238 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2432 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `1e0b2c53`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
